- **#762 Portable FBX export and round-trip:** Development: Complete. Automated and real Editor/MCP/Blender testing: Complete; direct pointer/keyboard UI stress remains pending. Exact live-scene or 1–256-root snapshots, transform-only excluded-ancestor shells, 10,000-node work bounds, project-contained binary `.fbx` destinations, normalized Blender settings, SHA-256 leases, literal confirmation, per-destination serialization, project-switch rejection, export/deletion fencing, generic hashed-evidence cleanup, post-conversion scene revalidation, transactional output/evidence publication, and the ordinary Model Importer/instantiation owner protect Codex CLI and Claude-compatible/MCPB clients. Focused shared/CLI/editor/UI/schema gates, complete Tools/CLI/Editor suites, builds, the 1,685-tool contract, ten XML pairs, a 10,000-operation lane probe, and a real nested animated/material scene through Blender binary FBX 7400, Assimp reimport, screenshot, save/reload, stale rejection, and residue-free cleanup pass. Unity FBX Exporter package/API/serialization and bit-identical DCC output are not claimed.

This document is the **single source of truth shared by two implementations**:

1. The **MCP server** package (`mcp/`) — registers each tool and forwards it to the editor.
2. The **editor** side (`editor/src/mcp/`) — runs the HTTP server and executes each endpoint against the live scene.

Both implementations MUST agree on the **endpoint name**, **input fields**, and **output shape** defined here. Do not rename endpoints or change shapes without updating this file.

This file and `mcp/specifications.md` are the authoritative MCP product contract. Goal: an AI agent connected to this MCP must be able to fully build a game (e.g. "make a Minecraft clone") — compose scenes, manage assets/marketplace, write and attach scripts, optimize via instancing, and verify visually via screenshots.

## Verified contract increment: Unity 6.5 VFX templates and batch release (#752)

- Completion: Complete. Testing: Complete. Four strict closed tools expose the deterministic searchable/category-filtered VFX template catalog, compile-checked `.npss` creation, exact live batch-release evidence, and stale-safe policy mutation: `list_vfx_graph_templates`, `create_vfx_graph_from_template`, `get_node_particle_batch_release`, and `set_node_particle_batch_release`. Exact SHA-256 catalog/runtime leases, query/category/page/name bounds, exclusive project-contained writes, symbolic-link rejection, compile-before-publication, rollback, closed schemas, and four annotations guard the Assets Browser/Inspector/editor/export workflow. The server exposes 1,622 tools. Focused 6 shared and 6 editor tests and complete 972/1,854/51 shared/editor/CLI suites pass; affected format/lint, full production build, ten XML pairs, bundled contract validation, and rebuilt Electron/MCP creation/release/rebuild/UI/error-free/exact-cleanup pass. Unity VFX Graph/VFXManager/Shader Graph package/API, HLSL/SRP, native GPU-batch, and binary identity are not claimed.

### VFX template and release lifecycle

1. Search `list_vfx_graph_templates` with an optional bounded query and category, then retain the exact catalog revision.
2. Create one project-contained `.npss` through `create_vfx_graph_from_template`. The graph must compile before an exclusive write publishes it; stale catalogs, traversal, symbolic links, malformed names, and unknown fields reject.
3. Instantiate the asset through the existing particle-system tools, call `get_node_particle_batch_release`, and retain its exact revision.
4. Call `set_node_particle_batch_release` with that revision. When enabled, disabling releases the live batch once and re-enabling rebuilds it once from the retained graph; failures publish exact error evidence and policy mutation rolls back.
5. Re-inspect after each state transition. The lifecycle is shared by editor preview and exported/full/additive loading and explicitly does not claim Unity VFXManager or native GPU-batch identity.

## Latest verified contract increment: Unity 6.5 serialization diagnostics and Project Auditor (#740)

- Completion: Complete for the bounded portable implementation. Testing: Complete for implementation and protocol. Seven strict tools expand the complete server from 1,528 to 1,535: `get_project_auditor_capabilities`, `get_project_auditor_state`, `start_project_audit`, `get_project_audit`, `list_project_audit_issues`, `cancel_project_audit`, and `apply_project_auditor_fix`. The compiler and auditor share deterministic `SER1001`–`SER1011`; background categories cover serialization, resolved deprecated/imported future-obsolete TypeScript APIs, GPU-only particle texture Readable waste, project atlas allocation, and active-scene unused regions. Ten focused and complete 941 shared, 1,799 editor, and 51 CLI tests pass; affected lint, builds/bundle, ten XML pairs, closed stdio validation, and the real Electron lifecycle pass.

### Project Auditor lifecycle

1. Read `get_project_auditor_state`, retain its exact global revision, then call `start_project_audit`. At most one job runs per scene; work yields between bounded phases and can be cancelled cooperatively.
2. Poll `get_project_audit` to a terminal state. Use its exact job revision with `list_project_audit_issues`; filtered pages are stable and never expose internal fix payloads.
3. Re-read global and job state before a mutation. `apply_project_auditor_fix` additionally requires the issue fingerprint and literal confirmation. Version 1 only disables CPU `readable` metadata for an audited GPU-only particle texture and never changes source bytes.
4. Rerun the relevant category to verify a fix. Completed jobs retain deterministic issue/result fingerprints; cancellation changes no project file.

All schemas are closed and bounded and publish all four safety annotations. Source discovery is capped at 2,000 TypeScript files, 2 MiB per file and 64 MiB aggregate; JSON documents, particle documents, atlases, frames, issues, returned pages, and retained jobs also have explicit limits. Project realpaths reject symbolic-link escape. This is Babylon/TypeScript behavior, not Unity Project Auditor, C# Roslyn, Unity obsolete-database, or package/API identity.

## Verified contract increment: Unity 6.5 Texture Inspector channel preview (#739)

- Completion: Complete for the bounded portable implementation. Testing: Complete for implementation and protocol. Two strict tools expand the complete server from 1,526 to 1,528: `get_texture_channel_preview` and `set_texture_channel_preview`. They expose RGBA/red/green/blue/alpha selection, grayscale-by-default and colorized display modes, effective original/alpha semantics, an exact transient revision, a 1,024-pixel decode bound, and explicit non-persistence/non-mutation evidence. Ten focused tests and complete 941 shared, 1,789 editor, and 51 CLI suites pass; touched lint, editor/MCP builds and bundle, ten XML pairs, real stdio contract validation, and a rebuilt Electron UI/canvas-pixel/stale/idempotent/restore/cleanup lifecycle pass.

### Texture channel-preview lifecycle

1. Open an existing supported image with `open_asset_inspector`, then read `get_texture_channel_preview` and preserve its exact revision.
2. Call `set_texture_channel_preview` with that revision and at least one of `channel` or `displayMode`. RGBA shows original bytes; a selected channel is made opaque for inspection.
3. Grayscale replicates one channel into RGB. Colorized tints red, green, or blue; alpha intentionally remains opaque grayscale.
4. Re-read before each external mutation. File/directory moves preserve the transient state; successful deletion clears it so a recreated path starts at revision zero. Preview state is session-only and never changes source bytes, sidecars, importer settings, runtime manifests, or build output.

Both inputs are closed and bounded and publish all four safety annotations. Path resolution requires an existing supported project-contained file and rejects traversal plus symbolic-link escape. The UI decode is capped at 1,024 pixels on each axis. `.mcp.json` and `.codex/config.toml` expose the same 1,528-tool server to Claude Code and Codex CLI. This is portable preview behavior, not Unity Texture Inspector API or pixel-renderer identity.

## Verified contract increment: Unity 6.5 Build Profile actions, installed-platform restart, and Swift iOS generation (#738)

- Completion: Complete for the bounded portable implementation. Testing: Complete for implementation and protocol. Seven strict tools expand the complete server from 1,519 to 1,526: `list_build_profile_footer_actions`, `invoke_build_profile_footer_action`, `get_installed_platform_restart_status`, `plan_installed_platform_restart`, `restart_editor_for_installed_platform`, `get_ios_project_generation_capabilities`, and `validate_ios_generated_project`. Existing `generate_platform_scaffold` remains the exact-revision write owner for both Capacitor and Swift projects. Complete suites pass 941 shared, 1,779 editor, and 51 CLI tests; focused tests, affected format/lint, MCP/editor builds and bundle, ten XML pairs, contract validation, live extension/platform scenarios, a real Electron relaunch/reconnect/reopen lifecycle, generated-project integrity, Xcode project listing, unsigned Simulator compilation, visible UI, and exact cleanup pass.

### Build Profile action, restart, and Swift project lifecycle

1. Page `list_build_profile_footer_actions` for a selected or active profile. Preserve its Build Profiles configuration revision and each action's exact extension fingerprint.
2. Call `invoke_build_profile_footer_action` only for an enabled, trusted, active, manifest-declared, target-eligible registration. Supply the exact configuration revision and fingerprint plus `confirm: true`; the host executes it with a frozen selected-profile context under a 30-second bound.
3. Read `get_installed_platform_restart_status` for one target and preserve its diagnostic fingerprint. Plan with that exact evidence, then call `restart_editor_for_installed_platform` with the single-use five-minute plan and `confirm: true`. The editor saves, relaunches, reopens the absolute project, and reports matching startup evidence after MCP reconnects.
4. Read `get_ios_project_generation_capabilities`. Generate or update an iOS scaffold with `projectType: "swift"`, iOS 16 or newer, and `syncNativeProject: false` through `generate_platform_scaffold`; preserve the returned scaffold revision.
5. Call `validate_ios_generated_project`, then run the normal Build Profile pipeline to copy the Web payload into the SwiftUI/WKWebView Xcode project. Remove or update through the exact scaffold revision and verify that user-owned files remain preserved.

The portable contract does not claim Unity extension/Hub/API identity, platform SDK installation, Apple signing/provisioning, physical-device deployment, App Store submission, or distribution certification.

## Verified contract increment: Unity 6.5 GUI interaction and Inspector collections (#737)

- Completion: Complete for the bounded portable implementation. Testing: Complete for implementation and protocol. Nine strict tools expand the complete server from 1,510 to 1,519: `get_gui_interaction_capabilities`, `get_gui_canvas_group`, `set_gui_canvas_group`, `list_gui_raycast_receivers`, `set_gui_raycast_receiver`, `get_gui_usage_tracking`, `set_gui_usage_tracking`, `reset_gui_usage_tracking`, and `get_inspector_collection_capabilities`. They expose exact-revision CanvasGroup alpha/interactivity/raycast policy, invisible RaycastReceiver assignments, local-only bounded usage evidence, and typed styled Inspector list/array capabilities. Existing GUI creation/retained-document and script discovery/value tools complete the lifecycle. Complete suites pass 941 shared, 1,776 editor, and 51 CLI tests; affected lint/format, builds, ten XML pairs, bundled contract validation, real Electron save/reload/stale-reject/external-mutation/exact-cleanup, and zero renderer errors pass.

### GUI interaction and collection lifecycle

1. Call `get_gui_interaction_capabilities`, create or instantiate a GUI asset, and preserve its exact authoring revision.
2. Create or select a container and set its CanvasGroup assignment; nested alpha and input/raycast policies apply hierarchically unless the child group ignores parents.
3. Create or attach an invisible RaycastReceiver, page assignments, and enable/disable it under the exact revision.
4. Opt into local usage tracking, inspect bounded counters/events, and explicitly reset transient evidence. Never treat it as remotely transmitted telemetry.
5. Discover `visibleAsArray`/`visibleAsList` fields and collection styles, attach the script, and replace bounded values through `set_script_exported_value`. The Inspector remains the visual add/remove/reorder owner.
6. Save/reload and reread the exact state; remove temporary scripts, nodes, GUI instances, and assets through their owning tools.

This contract provides portable Babylon behavior and does not claim Unity UGUI package, C# API, or binary identity.

## Latest verified contract increment: Unity 6.5 shared Addressables TypeTrees (#735)

- Completion: Complete for the bounded portable implementation. Testing: Complete for implementation and protocol. Four strict read-only endpoints expand Addressables from 31 to 35 tools and the complete server from 1,499 to 1,503: `analyze_addressable_type_trees`, `get_addressable_type_tree_build`, `get_addressable_type_tree_schema`, and `validate_addressable_portable_bundle`. They expose no-write savings analysis, verified build evidence, paginated schema nodes, and paginated decoded entry evidence. Catalog v3 binds registry, schema, bundle, asset, size, SHA-256, load-path, and membership identities; version-2 catalogs migrate at runtime. Full/update builds, pack modes, static redirects, local/export output, remote pointer-last deployment, and preview/export loading share the same bounded codec. Complete suites pass 930 shared, 1,770 editor, and 51 CLI tests; affected lint/format, XML evaluation, dependency builds, the bundled contract, and a real 35-tool Electron lifecycle with strict rejection, deployment verification, portable runtime loading, and exact cleanup pass.

### Shared TypeTree lifecycle

1. Call `analyze_addressable_type_trees` with a small page limit. It evaluates extraction even when the authored switch is disabled and labels that result `what-if-enabled`; page bundles until `hasMore` is false.
2. Enable extraction through exact-revision `set_addressable_settings`, choose group `bundleMode`, then create a full or update build.
3. Call `get_addressable_type_tree_build` on the returned project-local report path and preserve exact registry, bundle, and schema IDs.
4. Page a schema with `get_addressable_type_tree_schema`; validate each bundle with `validate_addressable_portable_bundle`. Treat any missing artifact, path escape, size/hash/identity mismatch, duplicate membership, or semantic entry mismatch as a failed build.
5. Deploy and verify through the existing pointer-last tools, or reload the editor runtime and prefetch addressed entries. Extraction is valid only when the emitted registry plus bundles are strictly smaller than the raw eligible inputs.

This contract reconstructs portable JSON semantics and does not claim Unity AssetBundle binary format, TypeTree API, or runtime identity.

## Latest verified contract increment: Unity 6.5 extensible 2D lighting providers (#730)

- Completion: Complete for bounded portable Babylon behavior. Testing: Complete for implementation and protocol. Strict read-only `list_light2d_provider_types` and `get_lighting2d_runtime` expand the server from 1,466 to 1,468 closed fully annotated tools. The first provides paged built-in/project Light2D and ShadowShape2D provider discovery with exact versions, defaults, priority, and node-data requirements. The second selects Edit, Play, or automatic target state and reports exact compiled-Play provider registrations plus measured lifecycle, geometry, timing, error, consumer, warning, and bound evidence. Existing strict component tools own authored Light2D/ShadowCaster2D data and the script-template lifecycle owns generated project provider registration. Focused tests pass 9 shared-runtime and 4 dedicated editor tests; complete suites pass 893/893 and 1,735/1,735. Dependency-ordered builds, affected zero-error lint, bundle/contract, real Electron WebGL2 shader/pixel execution, and the rebuilt running-editor eight-tool Edit/compiled-Play/rejection/cleanup lifecycle pass.

### Light2D provider lifecycle

1. Call `list_light2d_provider_types` for `light`, `shadow`, or `all`; page until `nextOffset` is null and preserve exact `id` plus `dataVersion`.
2. Add or update the singleton `light2d` or `shadowcaster2d` component through the strict GameObject component tools. A custom component must use a non-built-in provider id, the exact registered version, and bounded JSON provider data.
3. Register project behavior from TypeScript with `registerLight2DProvider` or `registerShadowShape2DProvider`; the `light2d-providers` script template supplies an editable example.
4. Call `get_lighting2d_runtime` in Edit for immediate authoring evidence or in Play for the exact generated bundle's registry and lifecycle evidence. `auto` chooses a ready Play target, otherwise Edit.
5. Treat unregistered/version-mismatched providers and provider callback failures as explicit invalid evidence. Stop Play or remove temporary components/scripts through their owning tools and verify cleanup.

## Latest verified contract increment: Unity-style Scripting workflow (#725)

- Completion: Complete for the portable TypeScript workflow. Testing: Complete for implementation and protocol. Ten strict closed additions expand MCP from 1,393 to 1,403 tools: `get_custom_script_template`, `delete_custom_script_template`, `get_script_debugger_capabilities`, `prepare_script_debugger`, `get_script_debugger`, `set_script_breakpoints`, `control_script_debugger`, `get_script_source_coverage`, `set_script_source_coverage`, and `export_script_source_coverage`. Every tool publishes all four annotations; exact manifest/configuration/coverage leases guard mutations and exports. Focused scripting tests pass 12/12; complete shared-runtime/editor suites pass 857/1,692; dependency-ordered builds, affected lint/format/XML, ten-pair evaluation, bundle/contract, real Electron all-tool lifecycle and visible UI, and direct Codex CLI invocation pass. Claude Code discovers the server while its model call is externally blocked by expired local OAuth.

### Script debugger lifecycle

1. Call `get_script_debugger_capabilities`, then `prepare_script_debugger` to start instrumented Debug Play.
2. Call `get_script_debugger` and preserve the exact manifest fingerprint and configuration revision.
3. Replace breakpoints through `set_script_breakpoints`; use returned resolved path/line/point evidence rather than assuming a requested line is executable.
4. Poll `get_script_debugger` for hits, safe instance fields, and trace pages. Use `control_script_debugger` for cooperative pause, resume, fixed step, or trace clear.
5. Enable or clear collection with `set_script_source_coverage`, inspect it with `get_script_source_coverage`, and export JSON or LCOV only with the exact manifest, configuration, and coverage revisions.

Arbitrary mid-expression suspension and evaluation are unsupported. The callback that reaches a probe completes before later script lifecycle delivery pauses.

### Cinematic audio-capture lifecycle

1. Read the document with `get_cinematic`, preserve its exact revision and fingerprint, and select an audio-enabled WebM or MP4 recorder profile.

## Previous verified contract increment: Unity-style Tile Palette (#723)

### Tile Palette lifecycle

1. Read the Sprite Map with `get_tile_grid_configuration` and the palette with `get_tile_palette`; preserve the returned grid, palette, map, and viewport revisions.
2. Select rectangular, isometric, point-top hexagonal, or flat-top hexagonal projection with `set_tile_grid_configuration` under the exact grid revision.
3. Configure `set_tile_paint_viewport` for a map or palette target, then call `apply_tile_palette_operation` with every exact revision returned by the latest read.
4. Use paint/erase/fill/pick/select/move/rotate/flip directly, or discover a loaded project registration with `list_grid_brush_types` and execute it using the custom operation.
5. Continue by hand in the Sprite Map Inspector and layout-aware viewport; both surfaces call the same operations and register whole transactions with Undo/Redo.

### Weighted 2D sprite lifecycle

1. Read existing rigs with `list_sprite_skins` or `get_sprite_skin` and preserve the returned `revision` and SHA-256 `fingerprint`.

Invalid values, changed bytes, and stale plans reject. The existing tools remain closed and fully annotated, and the server remains at 841 tools.

---

## Previous verified contract increment: authored PSD TySh ProportionalMetrics / OpenType `palt` (#502)

- Existing `inspect_psd_layer_extraction` and confirmation-gated `extract_psd_layers` preserve exact EngineData `ProportionalMetrics` state without adding a duplicate endpoint.
- Authored `false` and `true` execute exact HarfBuzz GPOS `palt=0` and `palt=1`, respectively, and override conflicting caller feature values. `null` leaves caller/default feature selection unchanged.
- The exact leased project font is queried for GPOS `palt` support. Unsupported fonts are reported explicitly; no guessed proportional-spacing fallback is synthesized and the exact authored feature request remains visible.
- Bounded evidence includes semantic state, support, effective OpenType features, exact glyph IDs/clusters/advances/offsets, HarfBuzz/font/source identity, and `bounded-authored-proportional-metrics-v1`. Invalid values, changed bytes, and stale plans reject. The existing tools remain closed and fully annotated, and the server remains at 841 tools.

---

## Previous verified contract increment: authored PSD TySh BaselineDirection and Tate-Chu-Yoko (#501)

- `inspect_psd_layer_extraction` and confirmation-gated `extract_psd_layers` preserve exact EngineData `BaselineDirection` values without adding a duplicate endpoint.
- Values `1`, `2`, and `3` map exactly to upright, mixed, and Tate-Chu-Yoko. Upright uses vertical shaping without rotation. Mixed classifies each Unicode scalar from Unicode 13 `Vertical_Orientation`, keeping `U`/`Tu` upright while horizontally shaping and clockwise-painting `R`/`Tr`. Tate-Chu-Yoko protects the exact authored run as one atomic horizontal block inside one vertical em cell and applies a deterministic bounded fit after tracking, manual kerning, and Tsume.
- Horizontal text reports `horizontal-no-op`; vertical execution composes with FontCaps, FontBaseline, StyleRunAlignment, decorations, BiDi, wrapping, and cross-style boundaries. Unknown values reject rather than being guessed.
- Bounded evidence includes each glyph's ID, UTF-16 cluster, line, Unicode class, rendered orientation, shaping direction, and rotation plus each Tate-Chu-Yoko block's exact UTF-16 range, glyph count, raw/fitted/cell advance, and fit scale through `bounded-authored-baseline-direction-v1`. Changed source/font bytes and stale plans reject. The existing tools remain closed and fully annotated, and the server remains at 841 tools.

---

## Previous verified contract increment: authored PSD TySh StyleRunAlignment (#500)

- `inspect_psd_layer_extraction` and confirmation-gated `extract_psd_layers` preserve exact EngineData `StyleRunAlignment` values without adding a duplicate endpoint.
- Values `0…5` map exactly to em-box bottom/left, ICF bottom/left, em-box center, Roman baseline, ICF top/right, and em-box top/right. The renderer aligns each line to its largest actually rendered cross-axis em reference.
- Em-box coordinates are exact; horizontal Roman uses the shared baseline; ICF and vertical Roman use exact `icfb`, `icft`, and `romn` coordinates from a bounded OpenType `BASE` parser. Missing, malformed, or unsupported metrics reject instead of falling back to guessed font geometry.
- Authored alignment requires HarfBuzz, participates in cross-style compatibility, and composes with BiDi, vertical layout, wrapping, baseline shift, FontBaseline, and decorations.
- Bounded per-glyph evidence includes glyph ID, UTF-16 cluster, line, reference style run and em sizes, glyph/reference coordinates, applied shift, metric source, script, BASE tag and coordinate formats, model, and exact source/font lease. Unknown modes, changed bytes, and stale plans reject. The existing tools remain closed and fully annotated, and the server remains at 841 tools.

---

## Previous verified contract increment: authored PSD TySh Tsume (#499)

- `inspect_psd_layer_extraction` and confirmation-gated `extract_psd_layers` preserve exact EngineData style-run `Tsume` values without adding a duplicate endpoint.
- Exact finite `0…1` values map to Photoshop's `0…100%` control. Rendering keeps each glyph's geometry unchanged and reduces the positive distance from its exact project-font ink bounds to the em advance on both primary-axis sides by the authored proportion; `1` removes those sidebearings, while `0` is a proven no-op.
- Horizontal and vertical HarfBuzz, BiDi, and compatible cross-style paths apply Tsume before tracking/manual kerning/paragraph spacing, include it in style-boundary compatibility, and use the reduced advance for justification, box wrapping, decorations, and final placement.
- Per-run evidence includes the authored value, affected-glyph count, aggregate leading/trailing trim and advance reduction, plus bounded per-glyph ID/UTF-16 cluster/before-after advance/leading-trailing trim records through `bounded-authored-tsume-v1`.
- Non-finite or out-of-range values, unsafe runs, changed source/font bytes, and stale extraction plans reject instead of being clamped or approximated. The existing tools remain closed and fully annotated, and the server remains at 841 tools.

---

## Transport & envelope

- Editor runs an HTTP server on `http://127.0.0.1:3712` (see `editor/src/mcp/mcp.ts`).
- The MCP server POSTs to `http://localhost:3712/{endpoint}` with JSON body `{ endpoint, ...inputFields }` (see `mcp/src/request.mts`).
- **Editor handler return contract:**
    - Handlers are `(scene: Scene, data: any, options: IMCPActionOptions) => any | Promise<any>`.
    - On success: HTTP `200`, body = the JSON value the handler returns (this is the tool's `data`).
    - On thrown error: HTTP `500`, body = `{ "error": "<message>" }`.
- **MCP `request.mts`** must return `{ json, text, isError }`:
    - `json` = parsed response body (object/value), `text` = pretty-printed JSON string, `isError` = `true` when HTTP status is not ok.
- **Most MCP tools** return `{ content: [{ type: "text", text }], isError }`.
- **Image-returning tools** (`get_screenshot`, `get_asset_preview`) return `{ content: [{ type: "image", data, mimeType }], isError }` where the editor handler returns `{ imageBase64: string, mimeType: "image/png" }`.
- **Verified stdio baseline (2026-07-28):** 910 tools are discoverable. Project render-pipeline profiles, baked-lighting scenarios, Adaptive Light Probes/APV, automatic baked GI, and all earlier Animation, Animator, VFX, asset, Prefab, rendering, collaboration, and editor schemas are strict/closed-world, carry all four safety annotations, and reject unknown fields with MCP `-32602` before editor contact. Destructive active-profile clearing, profile/scenario/probe deletion, and baked-GI cleanup require exact current leases plus literal confirmation.
- **Authored TySh manual-kerning contract:** `inspect_psd_layer_extraction` and confirmation-gated `extract_psd_layers` preserve exact style-run `Kerning` in 1/1000 em; bind each nonzero value to its run's leading renderable UTF-16 pair boundary; split shaping exactly there; apply the adjustment additively with tracking and font kerning; and return exact value, boundary, pixels, actual application count, glyph, lease, and `bounded-authored-manual-kerning-v1` evidence.
- **Authored TySh OpenType contract:** `inspect_psd_layer_extraction` and confirmation-gated `extract_psd_layers` preserve exact style-run `AutoKerning`, `Ligatures`, and `DLigatures`; automatically activate contained-font HarfBuzz shaping when any switch is authored; give authored values precedence over requested `kern`/`liga`/`dlig`; split incompatible cross-style and BiDi feature boundaries; and return exact authored/effective feature, glyph, lease, and `bounded-authored-opentype-features-v1` evidence.

### Node addressing

Nodes are addressed by `nodeId` (preferred, the Babylon `node.id`) OR `nodeName`. Endpoints that accept a target node accept both: `{ nodeId?: string, nodeName?: string }`. Resolve by id first, then by name. If neither resolves, throw `"Node not found: <id/name>"`. Materials are addressed by `materialId` similarly. Asset files are addressed by project-relative or absolute `path`.

### Visibility requirement (critical, from spec §"Important notes")

Every editor handler that mutates the scene MUST make the change visible in the editor UI:

- After creating/deleting/reparenting/renaming nodes: `editor.layout.graph.refresh()`.
- After creating/modifying a node: `editor.layout.inspector.setEditedObject(node)` and `editor.layout.graph.setSelectedNode(node)` where appropriate.
- After modifying the edited object's props: `editor.layout.inspector.forceUpdate()`.
- After creating/modifying assets (materials, scripts, etc.): refresh the assets browser (`editor.layout.assets.refresh()` — confirm method name).
- Prefer reusing existing editor "add"/mutation functions (`editor/src/project/add/*`, `editor/src/tools/*`) which already wire selection/gizmo/refresh, so behavior matches manual user actions and integrates with undo/redo where those functions do.

### GameObject component lifecycle

The component stack is versioned and ordered. Transform is a required virtual row at order 0. Persisted custom-data rows, existing behavior-script attachments, and a mesh's real PhysicsAggregate retain stable component identities; arbitrary metadata is never inferred as a component. All mutations require the exact SHA-256 fingerprint returned by inspection and register complete editor Undo/Redo snapshots. Copy changes only the shared editor/MCP clipboard and returns a second exact fingerprint required by Paste.

| endpoint                           | description                                                                                        | input                                                                                      | output                                                                    |
| ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `list_game_object_component_types` | List closed type, duplicate, dependency, toggle, removal, and optional node-support rules.         | `{ nodeId? XOR nodeName? }`                                                                | `{ version, types[] }`                                                    |
| `inspect_game_object_components`   | Inspect exact ordered rows, stable ids, adapter values, dependents, clipboard, and mutation lease. | `{ nodeId XOR nodeName }`                                                                  | `{ node, version, fingerprint, componentCount, components, clipboard }`   |
| `add_game_object_component`        | Add custom data, an existing `src/` behavior script, or a real Physics Body 3D.                    | `{ node selector, expectedFingerprint, type, type-specific fields... }`                    | Updated complete inspection.                                              |
| `set_game_object_component`        | Set enablement or type-specific Transform/data/script/physics values without replacing identity.   | `{ node selector, expectedFingerprint, componentId, type-specific fields... }`             | Updated complete inspection.                                              |
| `move_game_object_component`       | Move an authored row while keeping Transform and dependencies ahead of dependents.                 | `{ node selector, expectedFingerprint, componentId, targetOrder }`                         | Updated complete inspection.                                              |
| `remove_game_object_component`     | Remove the row and its real adapter state; dependents require explicit cascade.                    | `{ node selector, expectedFingerprint, componentId, cascade? }`                            | Updated complete inspection.                                              |
| `reset_game_object_component`      | Reset one row to deterministic type defaults.                                                      | `{ node selector, expectedFingerprint, componentId }`                                      | Updated complete inspection.                                              |
| `copy_game_object_component`       | Copy an exact component snapshot into the shared clipboard.                                        | `{ node selector, expectedFingerprint, componentId }`                                      | `{ copied, type, sourceNodeId, sourceComponentId, clipboardFingerprint }` |
| `paste_game_object_component`      | Paste values onto the same type or add a duplicate-compatible new row.                             | `{ node selector, expectedFingerprint, expectedClipboardFingerprint, mode, componentId? }` | Updated complete inspection.                                              |

### Meshes

ProBuilder-style component topology is shared by the Mesh Inspector and MCP. `get_mesh_topology` returns stable vertex, unique-edge, and triangle-face IDs for the current buffers. `bevel_mesh_edge` and `bevel_mesh_edges` consume those IDs once and publish one atomic replacement: selected edges must be manifold, multi-edge selections may be disjoint or share endpoints, and `segments` selects one to eight quadratic profile strips with mitered endpoint caps. Per-face amount limits, duplicate IDs, the 256-edge ceiling, skin-buffer safety, and all topology validity are checked before mutation; successful edits clear stale component selection and return `segmented-adjacent-miter-v1` counts/evidence.

`get_mesh_topology` also returns a deterministic exact-buffer `topologyFingerprint`. `loop_cut_mesh` requires that lease, reconstructs logical quads from the current triangles, crosses opposite edges through one manifold open/closed strip, and publishes `logical-quad-strip-loop-cut-v1` evidence. One to eight offset loops preserve complete arbitrary streams, normalized skinning, hard seams, submesh materials, and created-edge selection; every unsafe condition rejects before the one committed replacement.

`detach_mesh_faces` requires the same exact topology lease and atomically compacts one to 4,096 selected triangle faces into disconnected source submesh ranges or a separate transform-preserving Mesh Game Object. Every complete vertex stream, hard seam, skin buffer, face winding, and material range survives; detached faces remain selected. Complete snapshots drive exact Inspector Undo/Redo, while stale, duplicate/out-of-range/all-face, morph-target, unsafe-stream, excessive, invalid mode/name, and unknown requests reject before mutation.

Smoothing-group authoring uses the exact raw topology fingerprint plus a monotonic metadata revision. `get_mesh_smoothing_groups` derives compatible initial groups from current face normals and returns bounded group/face evidence. `set_mesh_smoothing_group` assigns group `0` (hard) or `1..24` (smooth) to exact selected faces. `auto_smooth_mesh_faces` creates isolated manifold angle-connected groups for the selection. Both mutations rebuild only required vertex records, average normals across coincident same-group records, orthonormalize tangents while preserving handedness, retain all other complete streams/submeshes/UV layout, and reject unsafe edits atomically.

Vertex-color authoring leases the exact raw topology, exact normalized RGBA stream, and metadata revision independently. `get_mesh_vertex_colors` returns derived-white/stream state, channel bounds, unique/painted/selected counts, last operation, and a bounded vertex page with direct, selected, or non-white filtering. `paint_mesh_vertex_colors` targets exact raw vertices or faces using replace/add/multiply plus opacity. Face isolation duplicates all complete stream records only across selected/unselected boundaries; raw painting does not change topology. Both paths update vertex-color/alpha flags and preserve selection, submeshes, smoothing, UV layout, skinning, and arbitrary streams.

Mesh-integrity authoring leases one exact fingerprint across every raw vertex stream, index, and submesh range. `inspect_mesh_integrity` returns bounded categorized summaries plus a filtered issue page without mutation. `repair_mesh_integrity` requires that exact lease and `confirm=true`, accepts a unique explicit subset of nine repairs, and publishes one atomic stream-preserving result. Open boundaries and disconnected components remain informational; ambiguous holes and non-manifold ownership are reported but never guessed.

Pivot authoring uses `unity-world-stable-mesh-pivot-v1` and one exact fingerprint over topology, transform, pivot, parent identity, and current world matrix. `get_mesh_pivot` returns local/world pivot state, transform origin, bounds-center and current-selection candidates, limits, and the exact lease. `set_mesh_pivot` accepts an explicit world point, raw bounds center, or unique selected vertex/edge/face average. It replaces only the translation pivot and compensates local position, then verifies unchanged world matrix, every bounded raw world vertex, bounded descendants, and topology before publishing. Camera-dependent, frozen, singular, arbitrary-pretransform, stale, malformed, and excessive states reject atomically.

Editable-source/runtime-export ownership uses `unity-editable-source-generated-export-v1`. `get_mesh_editable_source` non-mutatively returns the exact canonical source fingerprint/revision, independent export-settings revision, complete stream/index/submesh counts, detached generated preview, blockers, and last persisted artifact. `set_mesh_export_geometry` requires all three leases and changes only the optimization policy. Save keeps canonical binary geometry under `geometries/` and writes derived per-mesh artifacts under `generatedGeometries/`; direct export and CLI pack publish only generated evidence plus lean binary data. Optimization removes unused records and welds only complete bit-identical records. Morph targets retain source identity, and stale or missing persisted artifacts fall back to canonical geometry.

Spline camera-path timelines use `unity-spline-camera-path-timeline-v1`. Each timeline belongs to one spline-attached virtual camera, carries a positive exact revision, bounds duration to 0.01–86,400 seconds and keys to 2–512, requires unique IDs plus strictly increasing times with exact 0/duration endpoints, and maps time to normalized arc length using each source key's outgoing linear/ease-in/ease-out/ease-in-out/step interpolation. Authored Auto Play and Once/Loop/Ping Pong settings persist; time, direction, loop count, active segment, raw/eased amounts, and sampled path position remain transient. Updates and clear require the exact current revision, rejected changes are atomic, and detaching a referenced dolly is refused. The same Bezier-capable arc-length evaluator drives editor preview and full/additive exported runtime.

Terrain streaming uses `unity-async-terrain-tile-streaming-v1`. Each Ground belongs to at most one version-2 group. Create validates 1–4,096 unique Ground IDs; updates and deletion require the exact current revision. Distances use centimeters and must satisfy `distance <= preloadDistance <= unloadDistance`. Asynchronous mode bounds concurrency to 1–16, retries to 0–8, timeout to 1,000–120,000 ms, and unload delay to 0–600,000 ms. Remote bases require HTTPS except loopback HTTP for development. Direct editor export and individual/merged CLI packaging publish empty Mesh placeholders plus per-tile URL, SHA-256, byte length, and binary layout. Full/additive runtime state is observable as resident/queued/loading/loaded/unloaded/error with distance, attempts, fetched bytes, verification, load time, and error. Rejected mutations never advance the revision.

UV chart authoring addresses topology through logical position edges so seam identities survive required UV-only vertex duplication. `get_mesh_uv_layout` returns an exact fingerprint/revision plus paginated charts and bounded seam IDs. `set_mesh_uv_seams` requires that revision and atomically replaces, adds, or removes no more than 4,096 current edge IDs. `unwrap_mesh_uvs` also requires the exact revision, opens closed charts when requested, performs bounded harmonic relaxation, equal-density normalization and deterministic padded packing, preserves compatible vertex streams, and publishes `logical-seam-harmonic-relax-pack-v1` evidence under the next revision.

| endpoint                               | description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | input                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | output                                                                                                   |
| -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `create_primitive_mesh`                | Create a box, sphere, ground, plane, cylinder, capsule, torus, torusknot, skybox, or empty primitive. Reuse `editor/src/project/add/mesh.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | `{ type, name?, parentId?, position?, options?: object }`                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | created node summary (incl. `id`)                                                                        |
| `create_instance`                      | Create InstancedMesh(es) from a source mesh. **Must use `editor/src/tools/mesh/instance.ts` `createMeshInstance`** (not raw `mesh.createInstance`) so each instance gets a real `id`/`uniqueId`, copied transform/visibility/enabled, and collision/shadow wiring — otherwise the inspector shows no properties. Defaults each instance's parent to the **source mesh's parent**; `parentId` overrides. | `{ sourceNodeId?, sourceNodeName?, name?, count?: number, parentId?, transforms?: [{position?,rotation?,scaling?}] }`                                                                                                                                                     | `{ instances: [nodeSummary] }`                                                                           |
| `clone_mesh`                           | Clone a mesh. `cloneGeometry=false` shares geometry (spec: clone only when material must differ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `{ sourceNodeId?, sourceNodeName?, name?, cloneGeometry?: boolean }`                                                                                                                                                                                                      | created node summary                                                                                     |
| `set_mesh_material`                    | Assign an existing material to a mesh.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, materialId }`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | updated node summary                                                                                     |
| `set_mesh_visibility`                  | Visibility/enabled toggles.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, isVisible?, isEnabled?, visibility?: number }`                                                                                                                                                                                                     | updated node summary                                                                                     |
| `set_mesh_physics`                     | Add/update/remove a Havok physics body (gameplay). Mirrors `editor/src/editor/layout/inspector/mesh/physics.tsx`: creates a `PhysicsAggregate` with `getPhysicsShapeForMesh` default, sets `body.disableSync=true`. `motionType` static/dynamic/animated; `shapeType` box/sphere/capsule/cylinder/mesh; mass on body mass properties; friction/restitution on shape material.                           | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, enabled?: boolean, mass?, motionType?, shapeType?, friction?, restitution? }`                                                                                                                                                                      | node summary + `{ physics }`                                                                             |
| `get_mesh_bounding_info`               | Bounding box in local & world space + whole-hierarchy world bounds (for scattering/placement).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `{ nodeId?, nodeName? }`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `{ local:{min,max,center,size}, world:{min,max,center,size}, hierarchyWorld:{min,max,size} }`            |
| `bevel_mesh_edge`                      | Atomically bevel one manifold edge with mitered endpoint caps and a bounded segmented quadratic profile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, edgeIndex, amount, segments?: 1..8 }`                                                                                                                                                                                                              | updated buffers + exact bevel model/counts                                                               |
| `bevel_mesh_edges`                     | Atomically bevel 1–256 disjoint or endpoint-connected manifold edges from one stable topology snapshot.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, edgeIndices, amount, segments?: 1..8 }`                                                                                                                                                                                                            | updated buffers + edges/components/adjacency/counts                                                      |

### Lights & shadows

Adaptive Light Probe/APV tools share `editor/src/mcp/lights/light-probes.ts` with the normal Scene Inspector and `tools/src/loading/light-probes.ts` with exported full/additive runtime loading. `list_light_probe_volumes` provides bounded summary paging and opt-in exact probe/cell pages for one selected baked volume. `create_light_probe_volume` authors bounds, base resolution, adaptive levels, blend distance, priority, and explicit target mesh IDs. Exact-revision `set_light_probe_volume` atomically edits or invalidates bake topology; exact-revision `bake_light_probe_volume` computes deterministic bounded SH9 probes/cells against optional indexed geometry. `get_light_probe_runtime` returns runtime/plugin/shader/query evidence and can evaluate one target at a position. `delete_light_probe_volume` requires the current revision plus `confirm:true`.

Automatic baked-GI tools share `editor/src/mcp/lights/baked-gi.ts` with the normal Scene Inspector. `get_baked_gi {}` returns `configured:false` or the exact version/backend/bake ID/settings/limitations plus per-mesh original and baked material identities, UV channel, lightmap path, coverage, overlap, saturation, and ray counts. `bake_baked_gi` accepts optional bounded `meshIds` (maximum 128), `resolution` (16–256), `samples` (1–32), `bounces` (0 or 1), direct/indirect intensity, shadowing/bias/distance, dilation, `uvChannel`, and a contained project-relative output root; it rejects an existing bake and publishes only after all raster results and PNG files succeed. `clear_baked_gi { expectedBakeId, confirm:true }` rejects stale IDs, restores original materials from runtime identity or persisted snapshots, disposes only bake-owned resources, and deletes only the manifest-owned generated directory.

Realtime reflection probes share one persisted editor/export lifecycle. `list_reflection_probes` pages stable IDs/revisions, native cubemap readiness, capture/render-list settings, independent authored material assignments, importance, box-projected influence bounds, blend distance/intensity, and deferred-camera execution. `create_reflection_probe` validates every referenced mesh and material before construction. Exact-revision `set_reflection_probe` replaces render/material assignments atomically. Multiple probes may target the same material: the highest active importance wins, equal importance blends per pixel over the influence-edge fade, and unused weight falls back to the authored material cubemap or environment. `delete_reflection_probe` requires the current revision plus `confirm:true`, clears only exact probe-owned cubemap references, preserves unrelated authored cubemaps, and excludes runtime-only material clones from authored evidence.

Reuse `editor/src/project/add/light.ts`.                                                                                                         | `{ type: "directional"                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | "point"                                   | "spot" | "hemispheric", name?, parentId?, position?, direction?, color?: [r,g,b], intensity?, range?, angle? }` | created node summary |

### Camera post-processes / rendering pipelines

Per-camera effects. **Post-processes are per-camera**, so configuring one switches the editor's active camera to the target camera (so the effect attaches to it and is exported for runtime). Supported `type`s: `default` (DefaultRenderingPipeline: bloom, tone mapping, FXAA, image processing, vignette, DOF, chromatic aberration, grain, sharpen, glow, color grading/curves), `ssao`, `ssr`, `motionBlur`, `vls`, `taa`. Editor side: `editor/src/mcp/rendering/post-process.ts` reuses `editor/src/editor/rendering/*` (`get*`/`create*`/`dispose*`/`serialize*`/`parse*`) + `saveRenderingConfigurationForCamera`, and the preview's new public `switchToCamera(camera)`.

| endpoint                    | description                                                                                                                                                   | input                                                                  | output                                                                                        |
| --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `get_camera_post_processes` | Read all post-process configs for a camera (live if active, else the camera's saved config; null when disabled).                                              | `{ nodeId?, nodeName? }`                                               | `{ camera, cameraId, isActive, postProcesses: { default, ssao, ssr, motionBlur, vls, taa } }` |
| `set_camera_post_process`   | Enable/disable + customize a post-process for a camera (switches active camera first). Properties are the flat serialized keys of the corresponding pipeline. | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, type, enabled?: boolean, properties?: object }` | `{ camera, type, enabled, config }`                                                           |

#### Node Material visual code graph

The Node Material Inspector and MCP consume one bounded graph model. Graph reads expose at most 512 attached blocks and paginate at most 256 nodes per response; code is omitted by default. Numeric block ids are preferred because display names may be duplicated. Connection mutations validate exact ports, Babylon type compatibility, occupied inputs, and dependency cycles. CustomBlock updates replace the block atomically and retain all compatible wiring.

Optimization is a two-step exact-leased workflow. Inspection builds the active material and an isolated parsed clone, then returns a canonical SHA-256 fingerprint, settings, planned actions, and before/after block, serialization, shader-size, and compiler evidence. Mutation requires that exact fingerprint plus `confirm:true`, reproduces the plan on a fresh clone, and swaps material identity and mesh assignments only after successful compilation. Supported passes are unreachable removal, add/subtract-zero and multiply/divide identity simplification, multiply-zero simplification, finite scalar/vector/color constant folding, byte-equivalent constant merging, and comment/whitespace minification for reachable trusted CustomBlock GLSL.

| endpoint                             | description                                                                     | input                                                                                                                                  | output                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `get_node_material_code_graph`       | Read the bounded visual port graph, edges, reachability, and exact fingerprint. | `{ materialId, query?, offset?, limit?, includeCode? }`                                                                                | Paginated nodes, typed ports, edges, counts, reachability, and fingerprint.                |
| `connect_node_material_blocks`       | Connect exact ports with type, occupancy, replacement, and cycle validation.    | `{ materialId, sourceBlockId? or sourceBlockName?, sourceOutput, targetBlockId? or targetBlockName?, targetInput, replace? }`          | Added edge and optional replaced-source evidence.                                          |
| `disconnect_node_material_blocks`    | Disconnect one target input with optional exact stale-source guards.            | `{ materialId, targetBlockId? or targetBlockName?, targetInput, sourceBlockId? or sourceBlockName?, sourceOutput? }`                   | Removed edge or stable already-disconnected evidence.                                      |
| `set_node_material_custom_block`     | Replace CustomBlock code/signature/stage while preserving compatible wiring.    | `{ materialId, blockId? or name?, functionName?, code?, target? }`                                                                     | Updated complete block summary and graph fingerprint.                                      |
| `inspect_node_material_optimization` | Compile an isolated optimization dry run and issue an exact lease.              | `{ materialId, settings?: { stripUnreachable?, simplifyIdentityMath?, foldConstants?, mergeDuplicateConstants?, minifyCustomCode? } }` | Exact fingerprint, planned actions, settings, and before/after compile/statistic evidence. |
| `optimize_node_material_graph`       | Compile and atomically publish the exact inspected optimization.                | `{ materialId, expectedFingerprint, settings?: same optimization settings, confirm:true }`                                             | Applied actions, resulting counts, shader evidence, and replacement fingerprint.           |

### Tilemap Collider 2D

All five tools use closed schemas and the same persisted `unity-tilemap-collider-2d-v1` backend as the Sprite Map Inspector. A configuration has independent exact configuration, generated-geometry, and source-map revisions. Synchronous mode rebuilds after normal editor, palette, rule-tile, viewport, or MCP tile mutations; manual mode records bounded pending changes until explicit generation. Incremental generation reuses stable owned nodes while the changed-cell count is no greater than `maxTileChangeCount`; forced or over-threshold generation atomically replaces the complete owned set.

Per-atlas-frame collider types are `none`, full-cell `grid`, or normalized compound `sprite` contours with holes. Composite operations are `none`, occupancy `merge`, `intersect`, `difference`, or `flip`; output can be solid polygons/boxes or boundary outlines. Optional bounded Delaunay edge legalization preserves the decomposed filled domain and reports exact flip evidence. Physics settings include contained material identity, trigger/effector participation, friction, restitution, collision layer, override priority, Include/Exclude, Force Send/Receive, Contact Capture, and Callback masks. Calls validate no more than 65,536 expanded input or output bodies and clean only generator-owned resources.

| endpoint                        | description                                                                                   | input                                                                                                                                                  | output                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `get_tile_collider_generator`   | Read complete settings, revisions, pending changes, stable nodes, and last geometry evidence. | `{ mapNodeId? or mapNodeName? }`                                                                                                                       | Map summary plus configuration, map/geometry revisions, pending count, nodes, and build evidence.        |
| `generate_tile_colliders`       | Create or exact-revision replace the complete configuration and generate bodies.              | `{ map identity, expectedRevision?, tileIndexes?, layer?, composite/geometry/generation settings?, per-frame types/shapes?, physics/layer settings? }` | Published configuration plus full-build created/removed counts and exact box/polygon/edge evidence.      |
| `set_tile_collider_generator`   | Exact-revision update selected settings; synchronous updates rebuild atomically.              | `{ map identity, expectedRevision, update: { filters?, composite/geometry/generation?, per-frame types/shapes?, physics/layer settings? } }`           | Incremented configuration or manual pending state plus current generated-node evidence.                  |
| `refresh_tile_colliders`        | Generate pending geometry incrementally or force complete owned-body replacement.             | `{ map identity, expectedRevision, forceFull? }`                                                                                                       | Full/incremental mode, changed/reused/created/removed counts, generated revision, and geometry evidence. |
| `clear_tile_collider_generator` | Exact-revision delete configuration, owned helper nodes, and generated 2D bodies.             | `{ map identity, expectedRevision }`                                                                                                                   | Exact cleared state and removed body count; painted cells remain unchanged.                              |

| `list_sprite_shape_profiles`  | List reusable profiles with exact revisions, assignments, range counts, and usage.              | `{ offset?, limit? }`                                                                       | Bounded page plus total profile count.                                             |
| `get_sprite_shape_profile`    | Read one complete exact-revision profile.                                                       | `{ profileId? or profileName? }`                                                            | Textures, colors, PPU, border-safe UV flag, angle ranges, revision, and usage.     |
| `create_sprite_shape_profile` | Create a reusable contained profile.                                                            | `{ id?, name?, edgeTexturePath?, fillTexturePath?, colors?, pixelsPerUnit?, angleRanges? }` | Complete profile at revision 1.                                                    |
| `set_sprite_shape_profile`    | Exact-revision update a profile and atomically rebuild every dependent shape.                   | `{ profileId? or profileName?, expectedRevision, update }`                                  | Updated profile, incremented revision, and dependent rebuild evidence.             |
| `delete_sprite_shape_profile` | Delete an exact-revision profile only when unused.                                              | `{ profileId? or profileName?, expectedRevision }`                                          | Exact deleted identity; referenced profiles reject.                                |
| `list_sprite_shapes`          | List editable Sprite Shape controllers and generated evidence.                                  | `{ offset?, limit? }`                                                                       | Bounded page plus total shape count.                                               |
| `get_sprite_shape`            | Read one controller, spline, profile, revision, generated mesh, and collider evidence.          | `{ nodeId? or nodeName? }`                                                                  | Complete controller/profile/generated snapshot.                                    |
| `create_sprite_shape`         | Create a hierarchy-visible generated Sprite Shape using an existing or automatic profile.       | `{ name?, parentId?, parentName?, profileId?, profileName?, definition? }`                  | Created mesh, revision 1 definition, profile, generated geometry, and collision.   |
| `set_sprite_shape`            | Exact-revision update spline, profile, UV/fill/optimization/tangent, or collider configuration. | `{ nodeId? or nodeName?, expectedRevision, update }`                                        | Incremented controller definition plus rebuilt geometry/profile/collider evidence. |
| `delete_sprite_shape`         | Delete one exact-revision shape and only its owned generated resources.                         | `{ nodeId? or nodeName?, expectedRevision }`                                                | Deleted shape identity; reusable profiles remain.                                  |

| TySh `textRenders` contract                    | Shared by `inspect_psd_layer_extraction` and `extract_psd_layers`. Uniform and exact authored-style modes retain their project-font data and run ownership. Authored runs execute font, size, tracking, RGBA, faux bold/italic, horizontal/vertical scale, baseline shift, underline, strikethrough, and exact `NoBreak` spans. Exact authored paragraph runs execute all seven Photoshop justification modes, first/start/end indents, before/after spacing, and authored auto-leading through `bounded-authored-paragraph-run-layout-v1`. Exact box text can additionally opt into `applyAuthoredBoxLayout: true`; `bounded-authored-box-text-layout-v1` executes authored `ShapeType`/`BoxBounds`, whitespace/tab/authored-hyphen wrapping, `bounded-authored-no-break-v1` suppression strictly inside protected style spans, hard-break preservation, and terminal-line justification across every legacy/shaped horizontal/vertical path. Authored `AutoHyphenate`, style-run `Language`, `HyphenatedWordSize`, `PreHyphen`, `PostHyphen`, `ConsecutiveHyphens`, `Zone`, and `EveryLineComposer` execute automatically in that mode: supported Photoshop language indices use bounded static Liang dictionaries without breaking surrogate pairs or shaped clusters, and unsupported requested languages reject. The single-line composer is deterministic and greedy; the every-line composer globally optimizes bounded paragraph breaks. Horizontal text supports legacy codepoint layout or optional HarfBuzz GSUB/GPOS. Authored vertical TySh always uses HarfBuzz top-to-bottom shaping, exact vertical advances/offsets/features, top/center/bottom primary-axis justification, and right-to-left newline-separated columns. `bidirectional: true` resolves bounded Unicode 13 UAX #9 visual runs; matching-font authored runs can join with cluster-owned painting. `applyAuthoredWarp: true` executes recognized analytical presets or exact bounded tensor/piecewise-quilt custom envelopes after glyph rasterization. Tensor/quilt orders, the complete control lattice, patch counts, exact authored or uniform source slices, tessellation, expanded bounds, and model join the lease. System fallback, guessed language substitution, guessed font substitution, unknown orientation, malformed-envelope approximation, and unsupported presets are forbidden. | `{ layerIndex, text?, fontPath, fontSize?, lineHeight?, tracking?, justification?, offsetX?, offsetY?, color?, useAuthoredStyleRuns?, styleRunFontBindings?: [{ fontIndex, fontPath }], applyAuthoredBoxLayout?, applyAuthoredWarp?, shaping?: { direction: "ltr"/"rtl", bidirectional?, joinAcrossStyleRuns?, script?, language?, features?: [{ tag, value }] } }`                                                                                                                                        | Exact orientation, column count/advance, style and paragraph run ranges, style language, seven-way paragraph justification, line indices, indents, spacing, applied line height/source, auto-leading, exact hyphenation word/prefix/suffix/consecutive/zone values, inserted-hyphen counts/languages, protected-run and prevented-break counts plus per-run no-break state, single-line/every-line composer model, exact per-line UTF-16 ranges/advance/span/overflow, `bounded-liang-pattern-hyphenation-v1`, `bounded-dictionary-hyphenation-wrap-v1`, `bounded-authored-box-hyphenation-v1`, `bounded-authored-paragraph-run-layout-v1`, `bounded-authored-box-text-layout-v1`, font identities/paths/hashes/bytes, scale, baseline, decorations/thickness, `bounded-authored-character-style-v1`, boundary model, cross-style ownership, HarfBuzz/BiDi/Unicode versions, visual runs, glyph IDs/clusters/flags/advances/offsets, ink/clipping/masks, preset or tensor/quilt warp kind/orders/points/patches/slices/tessellation/bounds/model, and lease participation. |

Every containing embedded PSD-v1 document is rebuilt and validated through `bounded-recursive-smart-object-payload-replacement-v1`. PSD-stored paths and aliases are never followed. ### Particle systems

| endpoint                            | description                                                                                                                     | input                                                                                                                                    | output                          |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- |

| `instantiate_particle_system`       | Instantiate a `.npss` asset or create a default CPU, GPU, or buildable Node Particle graph in the scene.                        | `{ path?, type?: "standard"                                                                                                              | "gpu"                           | "node", name?, emitterNodeId?, emitterNodeName?, position? }` | created node/emitter summary |
| `delete_particle_system`            | Dispose one CPU/GPU system and cascade its events, collision/vector fields, and incoming proximity references.                  | `{ particleSystemId?, particleSystemName? }`                                                                                             | exact deleted id/name           |
| `set_particle_system_playing`       | Start/stop a CPU/GPU system and optionally execute bounded deterministic update-only verification steps.                        | `{ particleSystemId?, particleSystemName?, playing, simulationSteps?: 1..120 }`                                                          | runtime/readiness/step evidence |
| `get_particle_collision_planes`     | Read persisted CPU/native-GPU planes plus exact execution backend, shader, readiness, and dispatch evidence.                    | `{ particleSystemId?, particleSystemName? }`                                                                                             | planes and runtime evidence     |
| `set_particle_collision_planes`     | Replace up to eight CPU/native-GPU simulation-space planes with projection/reflection/restitution.                              | `{ particleSystemId?, particleSystemName?, planes }`                                                                                     | planes and runtime evidence     |

| `set_gpu_particle_interactions`     | Atomically configure bounded native WebGPU pairwise collision; WebGL2 reports explicit transform-feedback limits.               | `{ particleSystemId?, particleSystemName?, enabled?, radius?, restitution?, separationStrength?, maximumParticles?, maximumNeighbors? }` | configuration/runtime evidence  |

| `connect_node_particle_blocks`      | Atomically connect exact compatible ports with occupied-input replacement and explicit cycle rejection.                         | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, sourceBlockId?/sourceBlockName?, sourceOutput, targetBlockId?/targetBlockName?, targetInput, replace? }`          | refreshed graph summary         |
| `disconnect_node_particle_blocks`   | Atomically disconnect an exact input with optional stale source guards.                                                         | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, targetBlockId?/targetBlockName?, targetInput, sourceBlockId?/sourceBlockName?, sourceOutput? }`                   | refreshed graph summary         |
