### Sounds (audio ambience & spatial sound — required by prompts: "sounds of nature", "people talking in the market place")

Audio is authored as `SoundNode`s in the scene graph (a `TransformNode` subclass; `editor/src/editor/nodes/sound.ts`). A `SoundNode` wraps native Babylon AudioV2 playback loaded from either a native project clip or a `.audio-generator.json` asset via `setSoundAbsolutePath(absolutePath)`. Persisted/serialized properties: `soundRelativePath`, `volume` (0..1), `isSpatial` (3D positional sound attached to the node's transform), `autoUpdateSpatial`, `maxDistance`, `distanceModel` (`"linear"|"inverse"|"exponential"`), `panningModel` (`"HRTF"|"equalpower"`). Spatial sounds are positioned by the node's transform, so parent/position the SoundNode where the sound should emit (e.g. a fountain, a market stall). Generated audio uses the same native mixer/spatial graph as clips: static graphs materialize an AudioBuffer and streaming graphs feed a bounded AudioWorklet ring.

| endpoint            | description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | input                                                                                                                                          | output                         |
| ------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------ | --------------- | -------------------- |
| `list_sound_assets` | List `.mp3/.ogg/.wav` sound assets in the project.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `{}`                                                                                                                                           | `{ assets: [{ name, path }] }` |
| `create_sound`      | Create a `SoundNode` in the scene from a sound asset and load it. Set `spatial: true` (default) + `parentId/parentName`/`position` for 3D positional ambience emitted at a place; `spatial: false` for a global 2D ambience/music bed. Values in editor units (cm). After creation: refresh graph, select node, set edited object. | `{ path, name?, parentId?, parentName?, position?: [x,y,z], volume?: number, spatial?: boolean, maxDistance?: number, distanceModel?: "linear" | "inverse"                      | "exponential", panningModel?: "HRTF" | "equalpower" }` | created node summary |

### Scriptable Audio Generator assets

Audio Generator graphs are portable version-1 JSON assets with bounded typed nodes/edges, deterministic seed/sample format, one output, strict acyclic topology, and optional project-registered custom DSP callbacks. `audioClip` leaves accept only supported project-relative native clips. Reads expose both the exact graph-byte `fingerprint` used for mutation leases and `runtimeFingerprint`, which also hashes every exact leaf byte and size. Missing/deferred leaves make `buildReady: false`; editor Generate and CLI pack reject missing, excluded, escaped, oversized, or non-file leaves. Create/update writes are canonical and atomic; update/delete require the exact current fingerprint.

| Tool                         | Purpose                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------- |
| `list_audio_generators`      | Page/filter indexed generator assets and dependency/build evidence.                                                                                                           |
| `list_audio_generator_types` | Discover built-in/project node metadata, defaults, seeking support, and hard limits.                                                                                          |
| `get_audio_generator`        | Read the canonical graph, revision, exact fingerprints, dependencies, and build readiness.                                                                                    |
| `create_audio_generator`     | Create a supplied complete graph or a default audible oscillator graph.                                                                                                       |
| `set_audio_generator`        | Exact-fingerprint complete graph replacement with one revision advance.                                                                                                       |
| `delete_audio_generator`     | Exact-fingerprint rollback-safe asset/metadata deletion.                                                                                                                      |
| `validate_audio_generator`   | Validate one disk path with dependencies or one unsaved candidate graph without mutation.                                                                                     |
| `set_sound_properties`       | Update properties of an existing `SoundNode`. Only provided fields are applied. Resolve node by id/name; throw if it is not a `SoundNode`. `forceUpdate` the inspector after. | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, volume?, spatial?, maxDistance?, distanceModel?, panningModel?, autoUpdateSpatial? }` | updated node summary |

### Animations (animation groups — required by prompts: "idle animations", "an animation of opening", "animated meshes")

Animations live as Babylon `AnimationGroup`s on the scene (`scene.animationGroups`), visible/playable in the editor's Animation Groups inspector (`editor/src/editor/layout/inspector/scene/animation-groups.tsx`) and exported with the scene. Two sources: (1) animation groups imported with a mesh asset (e.g. a glTF character's idle/walk clips) — list and preview them; (2) keyframe animations authored by the agent (e.g. a door that swings open) — `create_animation` builds a Babylon `Animation`, wraps it in a named `AnimationGroup` via `addTargetedAnimation`, and calls `scene.addAnimationGroup`. BEHAVIOR scripts then play the right group at runtime (e.g. `scene.getAnimationGroupByName(name)?.play()` when the player is close). After mutating groups: `editor.layout.inspector.forceUpdate()` (and graph refresh where the inspector shows the scene).

For `create_animation`, infer the Babylon `ANIMATIONTYPE_*` from the resolved current value at `targetProperty` (dotted path supported, e.g. `"rotation.y"`): `number`→FLOAT, `Vector2`→VECTOR2, `Vector3`→VECTOR3, `Color3`→COLOR3, `Quaternion`→QUATERNION; fall back to the shape of the first key's `value` (number → FLOAT, `[x,y,z]` → VECTOR3). Coerce array key `value`s to the matching Vector/Color type. `loopMode`: `"cycle"|"constant"|"relative"` → `Animation.ANIMATIONLOOPMODE_*`.

| endpoint                 | description                                                                                                                                                                                           | input                                                                                                          | output                                                                |
| ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- |
| `list_animation_groups`  | List the scene's animation groups (imported clips + authored ones).                                                                                                                                   | `{}`                                                                                                           | `{ groups: [{ name, from, to, isPlaying, targetedAnimationCount }] }` |
| `play_animation_group`   | Preview an animation group in the editor (e.g. play a character's idle clip).                                                                                                                         | `{ name, loop?: boolean (default true), speed?: number, from?: number, to?: number }`                          | `{ playing: true, name }`                                             |
| `stop_animation_group`   | Stop a playing animation group, or all groups when `name` is omitted.                                                                                                                                 | `{ name? }`                                                                                                    | `{ stopped: true }`                                                   |
| `create_animation`       | Author a keyframe `AnimationGroup` targeting a node property (e.g. a door opening = animate `rotation.y`). Builds a Babylon `Animation` from `keys`, wraps it in a named group, adds it to the scene. | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, name, targetProperty: string, framesPerSecond?: number (default 60), loopMode?: "cycle" | "constant"                                                            | "relative", keys: [{ frame: number, value: number | [number,number,number] }] }` | `{ name, from, to, targetedAnimationCount }` |
| `delete_animation_group` | Remove an animation group from the scene (disposes it).                                                                                                                                               | `{ name }`                                                                                                     | `{ deleted: true }`                                                   |

### Animator controllers

Animator controllers use one persisted model across the editor panel, preview, exported runtime, Unity YAML conversion, and MCP. Every machine has a default `entryState` plus an optional ordered `entryTransitions` list. A conditional Entry transition has `{ to, conditions }`, targets a direct state or child sub-state-machine, and wins when every condition matches; if none match, the default Entry destination is used. Ordinary transitions may use a child sub-state-machine instance as `from`; that parent transition becomes eligible when an active leaf in the child reaches Exit. Synchronized layers inherit both transition flow and Entry routing and cannot own either.

| endpoint                         | description                                                                                                                 | input                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | output                                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `set_animator_entry_transitions` | Atomically replace ordered conditional Entry routing on the base machine, one independent layer, or one reusable subgraph.  | `{ controllerId?; controllerName?; layer?; subgraphId?; entryTransitions: [{ to, conditions: [{ parameter, exactly one of equals/notEquals/greaterThan/lessThan }] }] }`; `layer` and `subgraphId` are mutually exclusive; at most 64 routes and 16 conditions per route. | Updated controller description, or the focused subgraph result returned by `set_animator_subgraph`.                                       |
| `set_animator_entry_state`       | Set the default Entry destination and optionally restart through conditional Entry evaluation.                              | `{ controllerId?; controllerName?; state; layer?; play? }`                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Updated controller description.                                                                                                           |
| `get_animator_compiled_graph`    | Inspect the exact flattened graph used by preview/export, including resolved Entry evidence and child-machine exit routing. | `{ controllerId?; controllerName?; layer? }`                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `{ entryState, resolvedEntryState, entryTransitions, machineEntryTransitions, states, transitions, machineEntries, machineLeaves, ... }`. |

The `create_animator_controller`, `set_animator_controller`, `set_animator_layer`, and `set_animator_subgraph` schemas accept the same bounded `entryTransitions` field. All corresponding objects remain closed-world schemas.

### Prefab variant conflict resolution

Prefab variant conflicts retain the exact authored collection/index and owning variant path that produced them. Inspection compares the current recursively resolved base, the authored override, the current resolved preview, and optionally one exact live instance. Ambiguous stable identities are evidence only and are never guessed as mutation targets.

| endpoint                           | description                                                                                                                     | input                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | output                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `inspect_prefab_variant_conflicts` | Build a bounded visual three-way merge plan without writing the prefab or live instance.                                        | `{ path, nodeId? XOR nodeName?, targetIndex?, offset?, limit? }`; a live selector is optional and `targetIndex` requires it.                                                                                                                                   | Stable conflict ids, exact authored origin/owner, base/variant/resolved/optional-live values, safe choices, unique retarget candidates, inherited-conflict evidence, pagination, and exact `revision`, `baseRevision`, and optional `liveFingerprint` leases.                                               |
| `resolve_prefab_variant_conflicts` | Atomically apply one explicit safe choice for every conflict authored by the selected variant; partial plans and guessing fail. | `{ path, expectedRevision, expectedBaseRevision, nodeId? XOR nodeName?, targetIndex?, expectedLiveFingerprint?, resolutions:[{ conflictId, choice, targetNodeName?, targetParentNodeName?, targetPropertyPath?, targetCloneSourceNodeName? }], confirm:true }` | Clean revision/base revision, applied choices, and zero remaining conflicts. `base` removes the failed override; `variant` is limited to lossless component/redundant-removal conversions; `retarget` requires exact unique current-base identities. Writes roll back on post-publish verification failure. |

Both schemas are strict/closed-world. A live selector and `expectedLiveFingerprint` must be supplied together. Inherited conflicts must be resolved at the returned owner path before a derived variant can be changed.

### Prefab ownership and revision-bound reviews

Prefab review evidence is stored in the contained project-local `.babylon-editor/prefab-reviews.json` metadata file, never inside `.prefab` runtime content. Every record has a stable review id/path, stable owner/requested-reviewer identities, bounded comments and append-only decision evidence, request state/round, exact reviewed/current recursively resolved revisions, and a deterministic exact mutation fingerprint. Collaboration-enforced projects resolve supplied reviewer ids to authoritative enabled members and require a valid session; local projects accept explicit stable actor ids/names. Owners/admins manage requests and only assigned reviewers decide. Any Prefab or recursive-base revision change reports approval as stale without deleting its evidence.

| endpoint                        | description                                                                                                           | input                                                                                                                                                                    | output                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `list_prefab_reviews`           | List a bounded project review queue, optionally filtered by draft/inReview/closed state.                              | `{ state?, offset?, limit?, collaborationToken? }`                                                                                                                       | Paginated review summaries with current/reviewed revisions, stale state, stable participants, request round, approval summary, and exact fingerprints.                   |
| `inspect_prefab_review`         | Inspect one review plus a bounded comment page, or receive the deterministic missing-review creation fingerprint.     | `{ path, offset?, limit?, collaborationToken? }`                                                                                                                         | Review evidence, comments/page metadata, decisions, approval summary, current revision, stale state, and exact fingerprint; or missing-review path/revision/fingerprint. |
| `set_prefab_review`             | Create/update ownership and complete reviewer set, save a draft, close, or open a new exact-revision request round.   | `{ path, expectedReviewFingerprint, expectedPrefabRevision?, title, ownerId?, reviewers:[{id,name}], action, actorId?/actorName? or collaborationToken?, confirm:true }` | Updated complete review evidence. `requestReview` requires at least one reviewer and `expectedPrefabRevision` equal to the exact current recursively resolved revision.  |
| `add_prefab_review_comment`     | Append one immutable stable-author comment tied to the exact current revision and current request round.              | `{ path, expectedReviewFingerprint, expectedPrefabRevision, body, actorId?/actorName? or collaborationToken?, confirm:true }`                                            | Added comment and refreshed complete review.                                                                                                                             |
| `submit_prefab_review_decision` | Append approve/requestChanges/dismiss evidence for the current assigned reviewer; stale revisions cannot be approved. | `{ path, expectedReviewFingerprint, expectedPrefabRevision, decision, body?, actorId?/actorName? or collaborationToken?, confirm:true }`                                 | Added decision and refreshed review/approval summary.                                                                                                                    |

All five schemas and reviewer identity objects are closed-world. `expectedReviewFingerprint` must be refreshed after every mutation. Comment and decision bodies are bounded to 10,000 characters; reviews, reviewers, comments, decisions, file bytes, pages, and offsets all have explicit limits. Mutations are confirmation-gated and serialized through a stale-operation-guarded atomic same-directory publication. Review metadata is not an asset lock and does not change the Prefab source file.

### Prefab whole-instance overrides

Prefab instance targets use `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, targetPath?, targetIndex? }`. At least one of `nodeId` or `nodeName` is required. `targetPath` identifies an outer or nested source boundary; when that path occurs more than once in the ordered link chain, callers must also send the exact non-negative `targetIndex` returned by inspection.

| endpoint                            | description                                                                                                                 | input                                                                                                                                | output                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `inspect_prefab_instance_overrides` | Read a bounded, grouped comparison of the complete live instance against one exact source boundary without changing either. | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, targetPath?, targetIndex?, query?, categories?: ("transform"\|"component"\|"structure")[], offset?, limit? }` | Grouped entries with stable SHA-256 ids, current/source values, mutation availability, blockers/conflicts, counts, pagination, exact source `revision`, and live `fingerprint`. |
| `apply_prefab_instance_overrides`   | Atomically write selected live values and an optional complete structural batch into the exact prefab source.               | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, targetPath?, targetIndex?, expectedRevision, expectedFingerprint, entryIds, confirm: true }`                  | `{ applied, targetPath, targetIndex, previousRevision, revision, entryIds, counts }`. Hierarchy Apply requires a variant.                                                       |
| `revert_prefab_instance_overrides`  | Atomically restore selected live values and a complete structural batch from the exact source without writing the asset.    | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, targetPath?, targetIndex?, expectedRevision, expectedFingerprint, entryIds, confirm: true }`                  | `{ reverted, targetPath, targetIndex, revision, entryIds, counts, structuralChanges }`. Resources and hierarchy are published only after the complete operation succeeds.       |

Every input object is strict/closed-world. Re-inspect when either lease changes. `entryIds` contains 1–4096 unique lowercase SHA-256 ids from the same complete inspection. If any structure row is selected, every structure row from that inspection must be selected as one dependency-safe batch. Apply and Revert are confirmation-gated and serialized per source file.

### Prefab multi-instance overrides

Bulk inspection accepts exactly one selector: `targets` (1–64 exact live boundaries), `path` (all live boundaries for one project prefab), or `all: true` (all outer live prefab instances). A complete snapshot contains at most 8192 aggregate rows and returns a deterministic `batchFingerprint` plus exact per-target `targetId`, source `revision`, and live `fingerprint` leases. Pages must retain the same batch fingerprint.

| endpoint                             | description                                                                                                        | input                                                                                                                                                                             | output                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `inspect_prefab_instances_overrides` | Read a bounded comparison across explicit, path-wide, or scene-wide live boundaries without mutating assets/nodes. | `{ targets? XOR path? XOR all:true, query?, categories?, offset?, limit? }`                                                                                                       | Target summaries, aggregate counts, flattened paginated `{ targetId, entry }` rows, truncation state, and exact batch/per-target leases.         |
| `apply_prefab_instances_overrides`   | Apply selected exact rows with rollback-safe atomic or explicit per-target best-effort semantics.                  | `{ expectedBatchFingerprint, operations: [{ targetId, nodeId? XOR nodeName?, targetPath?, targetIndex?, expectedRevision, expectedFingerprint, entryIds }], mode, confirm:true }` | Atomic aggregate or best-effort per-target results. Atomic Apply requires distinct, dependency-independent source assets.                        |
| `revert_prefab_instances_overrides`  | Revert selected exact live rows with atomic value rollback or explicit best-effort structural results.             | Same mutation envelope as bulk Apply.                                                                                                                                             | Atomic aggregate or best-effort per-target results. Atomic Revert accepts transform/visibility and component rows, not hierarchy structure rows. |
| `open_prefab_bulk_overrides`         | Open the editor's shared multi-instance Overrides window after validating the bounded selector.                    | `{ targets? XOR path? XOR all:true }`                                                                                                                                             | `{ opened:true, targetCount, totalOverrideCount, batchFingerprint, selector }`; presentation only.                                               |

All top-level and nested objects are closed-world. Operations contain 1–64 unique target ids, 1–4096 unique rows per target, and at most 8192 aggregate rows. `confirm` is the literal `true`. Reinspect after stale leases or a best-effort partial result before retrying.

### Prefab Stage and property/component adornments

Stage settings are a complete version-1 project document. Every replacement requires the exact SHA-256 fingerprint returned by `get_prefab_stage_settings`; the editor and MCP use one serialized mutation lane, persist through project configuration, and restore the prior live settings if persistence fails. Context mode requires an exact live prefab node and optional exact `targetIndex`; asset-only opening is isolation mode. The existing whole-instance tools expose exact `component` rows for specialized Inspector controls and whole custom-metadata, script, and physics sections; no duplicate component-only tools exist. Physics rows materialize the live aggregate and Revert reconstructs the runtime body transactionally.

| endpoint                            | description                                                                                                           | input                                                                                                                                               | output                                                                                                 |
| ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `set_prefab_asset_nodes_properties` | Atomically replace supported name/transform/visibility values on up to 256 distinct stable source nodes.              | `{ path, expectedRevision, changes: [{ sourceNodeName, properties }], confirm: true }`                                                              | Updated asset summary and exact new revision; stale or concurrent writers fail without partial writes. |
| `get_prefab_stage_settings`         | Read complete persisted Stage mode, context appearance, environment, background, light, override, and Auto Save data. | `{}`                                                                                                                                                | `{ settings, fingerprint }`.                                                                           |
| `set_prefab_stage_settings`         | Replace the complete Stage settings document under an exact mutation lease.                                           | `{ expectedFingerprint, settings: { version: 1, mode, contextAppearance, showOverrides, autoSave, environment, backgroundColor, lightIntensity } }` | `{ settings, fingerprint }` after persistence.                                                         |
| `open_prefab_stage`                 | Open the shared visual Stage in isolation or against one exact locked live boundary.                                  | `{ path, nodeId? XOR nodeName?, targetIndex?, mode? }`; context/targetIndex require a live node.                                                    | `{ opened: true, path, mode, instance? }`.                                                             |

Compatible string, number, boolean, and vector Inspector fields consume the same whole-instance snapshot and stable row identity. The provider uses latest-request-wins loading so a late source-boundary request cannot paint stale adornments. Rotation-quaternion fields resolve against the serialized Euler `rotation` row. Apply/Revert always route through the shared exact-boundary backend.

### Marketplace (must be visible in editor — spec §marketplace, §important notes)

| endpoint                     | description                                                                                                                                                                                       | input                                      | output               |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | -------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------ | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- |
| `open_marketplace`           | Open the marketplace browser tab in the editor.                                                                                                                                                   | `{ source?: "polyhaven"                    | "ambientcg"          | "sketchfab" }`                               | `{ opened: true }` |
| `search_marketplace`         | Search a marketplace (driven through the editor's marketplace browser, not background APIs).                                                                                                      | `{ source: "polyhaven"                     | "ambientcg"          | "sketchfab", query: string, type?: "texture" | "mesh"             | "cube-texture" }` | `{ results: [{ id, name, source, type, thumbnailUrl? }] }` |
| `download_marketplace_asset` | Trigger a visible download into the project via the editor marketplace browser. Shows the same bottom-right progress toast (`ImportProgress`) the UI uses, so the user sees/cancels the download. | `{ source, assetId, resolution?: string }` | `{ downloadedPath }` |

### Scripts (TypeScript behaviors)

Reference: `tools/src/script.ts` (`IScript` = `onStart`/`onUpdate`/`onStop`). Script attachment metadata lives on nodes; see `editor/src/editor/layout/inspector/script/*`. Scripts must live under `src/**`.

| endpoint                    | description                                                                             | input                                      | output                                    |
| --------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- |
| `list_scripts`              | List TS scripts under the project `src/`.                                               | `{}`                                       | `{ scripts: [{ name, path }] }`           |
| `create_script`             | Create a new TS script with the editor's default skeleton at a path under `src/`.       | `{ path, className? }`                     | `{ path }`                                |
| `read_script`               | Read a script's content.                                                                | `{ path }`                                 | `{ content }`                             |
| `write_script`              | Overwrite/update a script's content.                                                    | `{ path, content }`                        | `{ path }`                                |
| `attach_script`             | Attach a script file to a node (writes the node script metadata as the inspector does). | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, path }`             | updated node summary                      |
| `list_attached_scripts`     | List scripts attached to a node + their exported values.                                | `{ nodeId?, nodeName? }`                   | `{ scripts: [{ path, exportedValues }] }` |
| `set_script_exported_value` | Set an exported/inspector value of an attached script on a node.                        | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, path, key, value }` | updated summary                           |
| `detach_script`             | Remove an attached script from a node.                                                  | `{ nodeId?, nodeName?, path }`             | updated summary                           |

### Agent automation scripts (`.js` run in the editor)

Distinct from behavior scripts: these are `.js` files in a root **`agentdata/`** folder, exporting `main(editor)`, compiled+executed INSIDE the editor (reusing the "Run script" pipeline: `editor/src/tools/compile.ts` `compileScript` → `require(outfile).main(editor)`, see `editor/src/editor/layout/assets-browser/items/javascript-item.tsx`). For complex/voluminous authoring (procedural geometry, scattering, bulk edits). Editor handlers in `editor/src/mcp/scripts/editor-scripts.ts`.

| endpoint             | description                                                                                                                                                                                                           | input                 | output                          |
| -------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------- |
| `get_editor_api`     | Reference for the `editor` mediator object (scene at `editor.layout.preview.scene`, graph/inspector/assets/console, conventions) + the `main(editor)` skeleton + a forest example. Derived from `/editor/src/editor`. | `{}`                  | `{ reference: string }`         |
| `write_agent_script` | Write/overwrite a `.js` script under `agentdata/` (name sanitized, `.js` enforced, no `..`). Must export `main(editor)`.                                                                                              | `{ name, content }`   | `{ path }`                      |
| `run_agent_script`   | Compile + run an `agentdata/` script's `main(editor)` (await async). Accepts existing `name` or inline `content` (write-and-run). Refreshes graph/assets after. Returns only a primitive script result.               | `{ name?, content? }` | `{ ran: true, script, result }` |
| `list_agent_scripts` | List `.js` scripts in `agentdata/`.                                                                                                                                                                                   | `{}`                  | `{ scripts: [{ name, path }] }` |

### Verification & utility

| endpoint         | description                                                                                                                 | input                                 | output                                                |
| ---------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- |
| `get_screenshot` | Screenshot of the preview for visual verification. Reuse `editor/src/tools/scene/screenshot.ts` `getBase64SceneScreenshot`. | `{ width?: number, height?: number }` | `{ imageBase64, mimeType: "image/png" }` (image tool) |
| `focus_node`     | Frame the camera on a node (helps screenshots).                                                                             | `{ nodeId?, nodeName? }`              | `{ ok: true }`                                        |
| `run_project`    | (Optional) Start the project dev/run process.                                                                               | `{}`                                  | `{ started: true }`                                   |

### Batch (spec §"Important notes")

| endpoint        | description                                                                                                                                                        | input                                                                          | output                                           |
| --------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `execute_batch` | Execute an ordered list of actions in one round-trip. Each action runs the same handler as its standalone endpoint. Stops on first error unless `continueOnError`. | `{ actions: [{ endpoint: string, data: object }], continueOnError?: boolean }` | `{ results: [{ endpoint, ok, data?, error? }] }` |

The MCP server exposes `execute_batch` as a single tool taking an array of `{ tool, arguments }`. The editor implements it by iterating `MCPEndpoints` in order. Make a single `graph.refresh()` / assets refresh at the end of the batch rather than per-action for performance.

## Networking/multiplayer tools (#726)

| Tools                                                                                                                          | Contract                                                                                                                      | Result                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `get_networking_capabilities`, `get_networking_configuration`, `validate_networking_target`                                    | Closed read-only configuration/capability/target audit.                                                                       | Exact version/revision, component readiness, provider boundaries, warnings/errors.                  |
| `set_networking_configuration`                                                                                                 | Exact scene revision plus a non-empty bounded nested patch; no credentials.                                                   | Complete next configuration and revision.                                                           |
| `start_gameplay_session_host`, `stop_gameplay_session_host`, `get_gameplay_session_host_status`                                | Loopback by default; LAN requires `confirm=true`; destructive stop requires `confirm=true`.                                   | Credential-free listener/security/count/error status.                                               |
| `create_gameplay_session`, `list_gameplay_sessions`, `get_gameplay_session`, `set_gameplay_session`, `delete_gameplay_session` | Exact scene/session revisions, bounded pagination/policy, confirmed deletion. Creation alone returns the one-time host token. | Transient lobby/player/ownership/manifest/relay evidence without retained host/reconnect tokens.    |
| `connect_networking_runtime`, `disconnect_networking_runtime`, `get_networking_runtime`                                        | Exact authored revision, memory-only credentials, confirmed disconnect, bounded trace paging.                                 | Compiled Play connection/role/ownership/peer/replication/simulation evidence.                       |
| `set_networking_ownership`, `submit_networking_input`, `send_networking_rpc`, `clear_networking_evidence`                      | Strict identifiers/tuples/JSON; owner RPC relation; confirmed evidence clear.                                                 | Protocol mutation result or credential-free runtime status.                                         |
| `start_multiplayer_play_mode`, `get_multiplayer_play_mode`, `control_multiplayer_play_mode`, `stop_multiplayer_play_mode`      | Exact scene/run revisions; 1–4 players; bounded per-player simulation; relational command schema; confirmed stop.             | Per-player isolated scenes, roles, assignments, metrics/errors, and deterministic cleanup evidence. |

All 23 tools have closed bounded schemas and all four MCP safety annotations. Network-effect operations use `openWorldHint=true`; destructive lifecycle operations require literal confirmation. The complete stdio server exposes 1,426 tools to the shared Codex CLI and Claude Code configuration.

The transport is authenticated WebSocket v1. It does not claim Unity Transport/Netcode package or protocol identity, raw browser UDP/DTLS, provider account provisioning, or vendor-hosted relay capacity.

External inspection/planning/execution/cancellation uses `openWorldHint=true`; every external mutation is exact-plan and confirmation guarded. The complete stdio server exposes 1,465 tools through the shared Codex CLI and Claude Code configuration.

The runtime uses Babylon `NullEngine`, exported scene loading, preserved server-game hooks, a fixed authoritative tick, authenticated `/session` WebSockets, health/metrics, Dockerfile, and graceful cleanup. Licensed console providers remain extension manifests: the editor does not bundle proprietary SDKs, devkits, credentials, platform-holder approval, certification suites, or vendor release services.

## Unity 6.5 release-delta tools (#755)

The final 6.5 release delta has twelve strict tools shared by the normal editor UI, Codex CLI, and Claude-compatible stdio clients:

| Area               | Tools                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Contract                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Animation Events   | `list_animation_events`, `set_animation_events`                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Exact per-group revisions, bounded callbacks/arguments/logs, shared preview/export dispatcher, and explicit error/warning/ignore missing-method policy.                                                    |
| Android builds     | `get_android_build_profile_plan`                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Fixed project-native None/Thin/Full LTO, XR adapter handoff, bounded Android Trace initialization markers, and an explicit prebuilt-library/Unity-internal boundary.                                       |
| Shader variants    | `get_shader_variant_collection`, `set_shader_variant_collection`, `clear_shader_variant_collection`, `trace_shader_variant_frame`, `prewarm_shader_variant_collection`, `validate_shader_variant_collection`, `get_shader_variant_collection_runtime` | Exact revision/fingerprint leases, rendered-effect tracing, bounded Babylon compilation, resolvability checks, and no native PSO/driver-cache identity claim.                                              |
| Linux ARM64 server | `get_linux_arm64_server_source_build_plan`, `verify_linux_arm64_server_source_build`                                                                                                                                                                  | Exact Build Pipeline revision and SHA-256 plan lease, public Node/TypeScript Linux ARM64 source package, fixed artifacts, strict descriptor/package validation, artifact hashes, and Node syntax evidence. |

All top-level and nested schemas are closed and bounded, all tools publish four safety annotations, destructive clears require literal confirmation, and read tools do not mutate or start produced games. Build Profile mutations reuse the existing exact-revision tools. The complete stdio server exposes 1,641 tools. This is portable Babylon behavior; it does not claim Unity API/package/source/internal-tool, IL2CPP, native PSO, driver-cache, or Dedicated Server binary identity.

## Implementation notes for the editor side

- Add handler modules under `editor/src/mcp/` grouped by domain: `scene/`, `nodes/`, `meshes/`, `lights/`, `cameras/`, `materials/`, `assets/`, `particles/`, `marketplace/`, `scripts/`, plus `screenshot.ts` and `batch.ts`. Register every endpoint in the `MCPEndpoints` map in `editor/src/mcp/mcp.ts`.
- Wire `initializeMcpServer(editor)` into editor startup — it is currently defined but never called. Call it once the layout/preview scene is ready (e.g. end of `Editor.componentDidMount` in `editor/src/editor/main.tsx`, guarded so it only starts once and ideally only when MCP/experimental features are enabled).
- A shared `editor/src/mcp/tools/resolve.ts` for `resolveNode({scene,nodeId,nodeName})`, `resolveMaterial(...)`, `toNodeSummary(node)`, and Color3/Vector3 coercion keeps handlers small.
- Respect editor units (cm) and glTF auto-scale (×100) already handled by the import path — do not re-apply scaling.
- Keep handlers synchronous where the underlying editor function is sync; return Promises where async (imports, downloads, screenshots).

## Implementation notes for the MCP server side

- Keep `mcp/src/index.mts` thin; consider splitting tool registrations into `mcp/src/tools/*.mts` modules imported by `index.mts`, OR register inline grouped by domain. Each tool builds a zod `inputSchema`, calls `notifyAndGetResultFromEditor(endpoint, args)`, and maps the result to `CallToolResult`.
- Extend `mcp/src/request.mts` to also return parsed `json` and a correct `isError` derived from HTTP status, without breaking the existing `text` field.
- Add an image helper so `get_screenshot`/`get_asset_preview` return `{ type: "image", data, mimeType }`.
- Update `mcp/manifest.json` `tools` array to list every tool with a one-line description.
- Tool `title`/`description` text is the agent's only guidance — write descriptions that teach the agent the workflow & performance rules from the spec (prefer instances over clones; non-shadow lights go in the clustered container; verify with screenshots; download assets via the visible marketplace; scripts go under `src/`).
- Pass server-level `instructions` to the `McpServer` constructor (2nd arg `ServerOptions`). These are surfaced to the agent once and are the place to state global guidance. The **#1 rule** must be: AUTHOR the scene with editor tools (real meshes/materials/instances/lights/cameras/particles/physics) so the user can hand-edit the result — do NOT clear the scene, create empty placeholder meshes, or generate geometry/materials/lighting/levels procedurally inside scripts. Scripts (`create_script`/`write_script`/`attach_script`) are for runtime BEHAVIOR only (input, game rules, AI, runtime spawning from already-authored assets). Then: **use `execute_batch` as much as possible** (one round-trip + one UI refresh; only split when a later action needs an earlier action's returned id), prefer instances over clones, clustered lights, cm units / glTF ×100, scripts under `src/`, physics via `set_mesh_physics`, collisions/deep props via `set_node_properties`, verify with `get_screenshot`. The script tool descriptions must reinforce "behavior only, not scene construction".
